# FR / GTTR — Artifact Manifest (artifacts NOT stored in this GitHub archive)

- Generated during read-only archiving on 2026-09-29. Nothing was deleted from the server.
- Paths are relative to `legacy_fluctuation_response/outputs/` on the research server.
- Copied into this archive: 2913 small files (~48 MB) — summary.json / REPORT.md / verdict CSVs / small figures / seals.
- NOT copied: 3160 files, 70.3 GB total.

## Category summary

| Category | Files | Total size | Safe to delete after GitHub verification? |
|---|---|---|---|
| v07_bank_DO_NOT_DELETE_YET | 113 | 4616 MB | NO — DO_NOT_DELETE_YET: consumed by ACTIVE server projects (optimizer_sensitivity F3 cells, muon_temporal anchor states) |
| G1a_bank_DO_NOT_DELETE_YET | 209 | 3779 MB | NO — DO_NOT_DELETE_YET: consumed by ACTIVE server projects (optimizer_sensitivity F0/F1/F3 anchors) |
| G1b_bank_DO_NOT_DELETE_YET | 209 | 14012 MB | NO — DO_NOT_DELETE_YET: consumed by ACTIVE server projects (optimizer_sensitivity F0/F1 anchors) |
| other_frozen_bank_REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE | 461 | 10534 MB | REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE: no active dependency known at audit time, but banks are the only copy of frozen states |
| dataset_KEEP_SERVER | 104 | 1587 MB | KEEP_SERVER: corpora (license/size); reproducibility requires them locally |
| checkpoint_raw_state | 1837 | 36186 MB | REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE: raw experiment states; verdicts are preserved in copied summary/CSV files, but bit-level re-verification would be lost |
| external_tokenizer_streams_REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE | 40 | 10 MB | REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE: external OLMo tokenizer streams (G1c line, abandoned) |
| RETRACTED_experiment_REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE | 1 | 1065 MB | REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE: artifact of a RETRACTED (do-not-cite) experiment |
| large_log | 105 | 3 MB | REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE: large run logs; small summaries already copied |
| other_small_but_uncopied | 81 | 166 MB | REVIEW_BEFORE_REMOVE: miscellaneous files not matching copy filters |

## Explicit DO_NOT_DELETE_YET markers (banks referenced by ACTIVE projects)

| Bank | Path | Size | Active consumers (as of 2026-09-29 audit) |
|---|---|---|---|
| v07 (56 states, 8 seeds x steps 46-82) | `outputs/e3_confirmatory/v07/` | 4.6 GB | optimizer_sensitivity F0/F1/F3 (anchors + tangent recursion), continuation_adaptation P4 C0 set (56 cells), documented in their configs SHA256 |
| G1a 26M bank (9 states) | `outputs/generalization_g1a_26m_v01/bank/` | 3.7 GB | optimizer_sensitivity F0/F1/F3 EARLY+MIDDLE cells (seed 2042/2043 step 26/64 anchors) |
| G1b 100M bank (9 states + theta q) | `outputs/generalization_g1b_100m_v01/` | 14 GB | optimizer_sensitivity F0/F1 (100M anchors) |

> These banks are frozen full training states (model + AdamW m/v/step + RNG, `full_state` v0.2).
> Although FR/GTTR itself is frozen, they remain input assets for ACTIVE projects on the server.
> Deleting them would break frozen-config SHA256 provenance of those active projects. DO NOT DELETE in the first cleanup round.
